# Progress Log

## 2026-07-03 — Codex direct implementation mode

- **Process update:** `codex-goal.md` is now the standing mission/process file for this branch. It supersedes the ACSP/cowork workflow for process only: Codex implements directly, historical ACSP sessions stay read-only evidence, and non-trivial work proceeds as smallest verified local slices with plan/progress/findings updates.
- **Milestone 0 checkpoint:** the accepted but uncommitted S4d-235..S4d-251 CMP UI migration backlog is now split into local commits (direct process contract, shared CMP UI foundations, Desktop shared theme, shared launch UI state/routes, shared utility screens, shared editor controls, iOS Templates UI XCUITest). No push.
- **S4d-252:** removed the dead `Action.ChooseImage` declaration and its `MainViewModel.process` branch after confirming it had no producer. This is deletion-only cleanup; `Action`, Android `Uri`, `ContentResolver`, `FuncTitleModel`, and raw `Any` payloads remain app-side edges.
- **S4d-253:** closed the S4d-234 iOS Templates UI XCUITest proof debt on the booted iOS 27.0 simulator. The first real `xcodebuild test` rerun proved the debt was valid: the fixture render/export and picker-open tests passed, but `testTemplatesSaveApplyDelete` failed because it waited for a stale seeded row and could not reliably reach/tap the Templates controls. The fix is intentionally UI-test/wiring scoped: wrap the tall iOS bring-up surface in a `ScrollView`, give the delete button a visible accessibility label, stop depending on a pre-existing seed row, use visible control labels for Save/row lookup, dismiss the keyboard before lower-screen taps, and scroll to off-screen controls.
- **S4d-253 proof:** `xcodebuild -project iosApp/iosApp.xcodeproj -scheme iosApp -destination 'platform=iOS Simulator,id=CF9CE125-D6B2-4D40-B634-56C5E5B65CF4' -derivedDataPath build/s4d234-single-ddata -resultBundlePath build/s4d234-single.xcresult CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO -only-testing:iosAppUITests/PickerFlowUITests/testTemplatesSaveApplyDelete test` passed **1/0** in 23.534s; the full `iosAppUITests` suite with `build/s4d234-xcuitest.xcresult` then passed **3/0** in 65.374s. I also viewed `build/s4d234-logs/manual-launch.png` from the simulator and confirmed the Templates section is visible/reachable. Boundary: this is iOS Templates UI proof only, not real PHPicker grid-cell selection, Android v2.10.0 parity, renderer work, or shared state migration. **Next:** run the Android emulator launch -> gallery -> editor -> adjust -> export smoke via AndroMeld and record screenshots/evidence.
- **S4d-254 (blocked evidence gate, not accepted smoke):** prepared the Android device-smoke debt but could not execute the required AndroMeld UI walk because AndroMeld returned `permissionDenied: MCP control is disabled in AndroMeld` on both `andromeld.wait` and `andromeld.devices.list` after launching AndroMeld.app. Proven setup: `./gradlew :app:assembleDebug --max-workers=8 --console=plain` passed (`BUILD SUCCESSFUL`, 84 tasks, current APK `app/build/outputs/apk/debug/EasyWatermark-2.10.0-21000.apk`), `./gradlew --stop --max-workers=8 --console=plain` stopped the daemon, and `android run --device=emulator-5554 --apks=app/build/outputs/apk/debug/EasyWatermark-2.10.0-21000.apk` installed/launched `me.rosuh.easywatermark.debug` / `ComposeMainActivity` successfully. This does **not** satisfy the requested UI smoke: no READ_MEDIA_IMAGES confirmation, gallery/editor walk, parameter-adjustment screenshots, or export screenshot were captured through AndroMeld. **Next while blocked:** continue Phase A code migration work; return to this debt as soon as AndroMeld MCP control is enabled.

## 2026-06-29 — S4d-233 iOS template bridge UI (+docs)

- **S4d-233 (implementation, accepted; commit `86e28f8 Add iOS template bridge UI`):** added the first Swift-facing iOS Templates consumer over the seeded Room path. `shared/src/iosMain/.../IosTemplateBridge.kt` wraps `TemplateRepository(buildTemplateDatabase().templateDao(), Dispatchers.Default)` behind flat Swift-friendly APIs: `currentTemplates()`, `addTemplate(content)`, and `deleteTemplate(id)`, returning `IosTemplate(id, content)` so Swift does not see Room entities, `Instant`, `Flow`, or `DataStore`.
- **Swift UI wiring:** `WatermarkWorkflow` now retains exactly one `IosTemplateBridge`, loads templates on launch, saves the current watermark text as a template, deletes rows, and applies a template by calling the existing `setWatermarkText(template.content)` path. `ContentView` adds a minimal Templates section with Save current / Apply / Delete controls. It reuses the existing workflow/render path; it does not add a separate template database, alternate editor state, or 1:1 UI polish.
- **Proof:** coordinator accepted the ACSP result after reviewing real diff/artifacts, then reran `git diff --check`, `./gradlew :shared:iosSimulatorArm64Test --max-workers=8 --console=plain`, and `xcodebuild -project iosApp/iosApp.xcodeproj -scheme iosApp -sdk iphonesimulator -destination 'generic/platform=iOS Simulator' CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO -derivedDataPath build/s4d233-review-ddata build`; all passed, followed by `./gradlew --stop --max-workers=8 --console=plain`. iOS bridge tests prove add/list/delete and seeded-row readback through injected seed bytes; Swift UI is compile/link-proven, not XCUITest-driven.
- **Boundary:** iOS Templates bridge + minimal UI consumer, **not** final Android v2.10.0 1:1 UI/UX parity and not a real on-screen Templates XCUITest. No Android production behavior, Desktop behavior, shared renderer/model, cmonet/dynamic-color, dependency, golden, or device change.
- **Docs closeout (this entry):** updated `AGENTS.md`, `docs/CONTEXT.md`, `task_plan.md`, `progress.md`, and `findings.md` so future ACSP briefs treat iOS Templates as Room builder + seed packaging + Swift bridge/minimal UI complete, with Templates UI automation/polish and final parity still deferred.

## 2026-06-29 — S4d-230 readiness + S4d-231 iOS templates Room builder (+docs)

- **S4d-230 (read-only readiness, accepted; ACSP `done/20260629-221818--s4d230-next-migration-readiness`):** selected the next smallest non-speculative migration slice after the Desktop batch-input lane. Current repo evidence showed Android templates already use commonMain Room with Android compatibility mode, Desktop has seeded/locale-aware templates persistence, and iOS had no production templates builder yet. Decision: add an **iOS empty-store Room builder** first, before seed packaging/default templates or Swift Templates UI.
- **S4d-231 (implementation, accepted; commit `278d585 Add iOS template Room builder`):** added `shared/src/iosMain/kotlin/me/rosuh/easywatermark/data/db/TemplateDatabaseBuilder.ios.kt` with `buildTemplateDatabase(dir, queryContext = Dispatchers.Default)` plus a no-arg `NSDocumentDirectory` overload. It uses Room KMP's generated `AppDatabaseConstructor`, `Room.databaseBuilder<AppDatabase>(name = "$dir/ewm-db", factory = { AppDatabaseConstructor.initialize() })`, and `BundledSQLiteDriver`. Because `Dispatchers.IO` is internal on Native in the current coroutines version, the query context is injectable and defaults to `Dispatchers.Default`. `shared/build.gradle.kts` scopes `sqlite-bundled` to the iOS target main source sets, matching the existing Desktop off-Android driver pattern and keeping Android on compatibility mode.
- **Runtime proof:** new `shared/src/iosTest/.../TemplateRoundtripTest.kt` runs under `:shared:iosSimulatorArm64Test` against a unique temp directory and proves the real `TemplateRepository` + `TemplateEditor` path: empty store, add/read, update preserving id/creationDate, and delete empty. Worker gates and coordinator reruns covered iOS simulator tests, `:shared:compileKotlinIosArm64`, `:app:assembleDebug`, `git diff --check`, no `sqlite-bundled`/`libsqliteJni` in `:app:debugRuntimeClasspath`, and daemon stop. Coordinator shell wrapper had two zsh bookkeeping mistakes after successful Gradle/no-leak commands; reruns confirmed the gates.
- **Boundary:** iOS templates **persistence builder only**. No Android behavior change, no Desktop behavior change, no seed DB/default-template packaging, no Swift Templates UI, no 1:1 UI/UX parity, no device/golden rebaseline. Next implementation candidate: iOS template seed packaging/default-template parity using the Android seed DB identity-hash work as precedent, then a minimal Swift Templates UI consumer.

## 2026-06-29 — S4d-229 Desktop Open-image multi-select batch (+S4d-229b docs)

- **S4d-229 (implementation, accepted; commit `4c895aa Add Desktop Open image multi-select`, 1 file):** the interactive Desktop window's **Open image…** button now supports native AWT multi-select and saves every selected supported image through the SAME sequential save spine as the S4d-228 drag/drop batch. Output format/extension follows the persisted prefs (S4d-128/S4d-228). Per-file failures continue the batch; the whole span is wrapped in `try { … } finally { busy = false }` with the `withContext` body in `try { … } catch { "Failed: …" }` (mirroring the S4d-228 r1 recovery); `lastImage`/`lastSavedFile` track the last successful save; `refreshPreview()` runs at most once after the batch. **Single selection still works** (a one-element list). No new helper/dependency/abstraction — the existing S4d-228 pure helpers and tests are reused.

Android/iOS/commonMain renderer/model/DataStore/Room, cmonet, dynamic-color, dependencies, resources, and goldens untouched.

Real modal multi-select **GUI** selection is a manual acceptance residual (out-of-process AWT dialog the headless path can't drive); the pure selection + collision-free naming are unit-tested. ## 2026-06-29 — S4d-228 Desktop multi-file drag/drop batch save (+S4d-228b docs)

- **S4d-228 (implementation, accepted; commit `c833aea Add Desktop multi-file drop batch save`, 3 files):** the interactive Desktop window's image **drag/drop** now watermarks and saves **every** supported dropped image instead of only the first. Added `shared/desktopMain` `DesktopSaveDecision.supportedImageFiles(files, extensions): List<File>` (pure, order-preserving extension filter), and rewrote the `DesktopWindow.kt` drop handler into a **strictly sequential** loop: `supportedImageFiles` → per-file `readBytes()` → `resolveUniqueOutputFile(outputDir, fmt)` → `DesktopWatermarkFlow.runSaveFlow(...)` → next file. The output format is read once per batch; per-file failures continue the batch (success/fail counts + first failure message); `lastImage`/`lastSavedFile` track the last successful save; `refreshPreview()` runs at most once after the batch (only when ≥1 save succeeded).
- **r1 (in the same accepted commit):** the batch span is wrapped in `try { … } finally { busy = false }` and the `withContext` body in `try { setup + loop } catch { "Failed: …" }`, so a setup-level failure (e.g. `userConfigRepo.userPreferences.first()`) resets `busy` and surfaces a status instead of leaving the UI stuck — restoring the old single-file drop's failure recovery.
- **Intentionally unchanged:** **Open image…** stays single-file, **Save as…** stays single-output (one chosen path can't name N files), and the headless `Main.kt` witness is single-image and untouched. Android/iOS/commonMain renderer/model, cmonet, dynamic-color, dependencies, resources, and goldens are untouched.
- **Verification:** `:shared:desktopTest --tests "*DesktopSaveDecisionTest*"` **14/0** (3 new cases: `supported_image_files_keeps_supported_subset_in_order`, `supported_image_files_empty_when_none_match`, `sequential_resolve_then_create_yields_distinct_names`) + `:desktopApp:compileKotlin` + `:desktopApp:run --args='--headless'` (witness output byte-unchanged: text `watermarked.jpg` 45,192 B, Image-mode 26,061 B, missing-icon guard intact) green (`--max-workers=8`, daemon stopped). `git diff --check` clean; exactly the 3 allowed files changed.
- **Correctness invariant:** the loop must stay **sequential** — `resolveUniqueOutputFile` checks existence only and does NOT reserve/create the returned path, while `runSaveFlow` writes its output synchronously before returning, so the next resolve must run after the previous write. A parallel/precomputed loop would collide on `watermarked.<ext>`.
- **Boundary (no overclaim):** release-grade Desktop multi-file drop batch, **not** Android v2.10.0 1:1 UI/UX parity. Real multi-file **GUI drop** end-to-end is still a manual acceptance step (drag/drop is an out-of-process UI event the headless path can't drive); the pure selection + collision-free naming are unit-tested. Open-image multi-select, folder batch, and progress/cancel UX remain future work. PR #358 stays Draft.
- **S4d-228b (this entry):** docs-only closeout — recorded S4d-228 in `progress.md`, `task_plan.md`, `findings.md`, `docs/CONTEXT.md`, and `AGENTS.md`, and updated stale "batch absent" language.

## 2026-06-29 — S4d-224 Desktop template default seeding (+S4d-224b docs)

- **S4d-224 (implementation, accepted; commit `c7959d0 Seed Desktop template database`, 3 modified + 3 new files):** the interactive Desktop window's templates Room DB now seeds from the existing English Android seed DB on first creation. The seed asset is packaged as a `:shared` desktopMain resource (`shared/src/desktopMain/resources/seed/ewm-db-eng.db`, byte-identical to `app/src/main/assets/ewm-db-eng.db`) so `:shared` does not depend on `:desktopApp` resources. Added `shared/desktopMain` `TemplateDatabaseSeeds.unpackDefaultTemplateSeed(output): File` to extract the resource to a writable file, and an overload `buildTemplateDatabase(dir, seedFile)` that copies the seed to the final DB path before Room opens it; the existing empty-store `buildTemplateDatabase(dir)` path is preserved for tests and callers that want an empty DB. Because Room KMP off-Android does not expose `createFromFile`/`createFromAsset`, seeding is done by direct file copy; Room validates the `room_master_table` identity hash (`72366f557b971b39675d0f26cbc46e0a`, already proven matching in S4d-143b). Wired both `DesktopWindow.kt` (real app DB under `~/.easywatermark`) and `Main.kt` (repo-local headless witness). Existing user DBs are **not** reseeded — the copy only happens when the target DB file does not exist. Headless witness is repeatable by deleting/recreating only its disposable `build/s4d143a-desktop-templates` directory before each run.

## 2026-06-29 — S4d-222 Desktop save output filename collision avoidance (+S4d-222b docs)

- **S4d-222 (implementation, accepted; commit `0eaafdd5 Avoid Desktop save overwrites`, 3 files):** the interactive Desktop window's default-name real saves (image drop, **Render & Save sample**, **Open image…**) no longer silently overwrite an existing `watermarked.jpg` / `watermarked.png` in the user output dir. Added `shared/desktopMain` `DesktopSaveDecision.resolveUniqueOutputFile(dir, format): File`, which returns `watermarked.<ext>` when free and otherwise picks the smallest available `watermarked_n.<ext>` suffix; the helper performs filesystem existence checks only and does not create/write/delete files. Wired the three default-name save sites in `DesktopWindow.kt` to use it. ## 2026-06-29 — S4d-219 Desktop durable watermark-icon persistence accepted (+S4d-219b docs)

- **S4d-219 (implementation, accepted; commit `97baa9d2 Persist Desktop icons in app storage`, `DesktopWindow.kt` only, +31/-2):** the interactive Desktop window's "Open icon…" no longer persists the user's original absolute icon path. It copies the picked icon into app-private storage `~/.easywatermark/watermark_icons/icon.<ext>` (preserving the picked extension) and persists THAT copy path, so Image-mode no longer hard-fails (`DesktopWatermarkFlow.require(iconFile.isFile)`) when the source icon moves/renames/deletes or the config opens on another machine — parity with iOS `IosIconPersistence` (S4d-116) at the release-grade persistence level.
- **Copy-then-prune (never clear-before-copy):** if the user re-picks the existing helper-owned copy (`selected.canonicalFile == copied.canonicalFile`) it is a no-op; else copy source → `incoming.tmp` then `java.nio.file.Files.move(tmp → copied, ATOMIC_MOVE, REPLACE_EXISTING)`, and only AFTER `copied` exists prune the other files → bounded to one helper-owned icon. An unsupported atomic move or a failed read throws → the existing `Failed: …` path with the prior copy untouched (pruning hasn't run). `IMAGE_EXTENSIONS` filter, mode→Image flip, and the S4d-198 preview refresh are preserved.
- **Verification:** `:desktopApp:compileKotlin` + `:desktopApp:run --args='--headless'` green (`--max-workers=8`, daemon stopped); `Main.kt` headless witness unaffected. The GUI icon-pick **click was not automated**, so end-to-end copy/durability is diff-proven + offline-algorithm-proven (one file regardless of pick count; re-pick no-op; failed copy = prior copy intact), **not** runtime-automated — a coordinator manual acceptance check. No stray writes.
- **Boundary (no overclaim):** release-grade Desktop icon-persistence durability, **not** Android v2.10.0 1:1 UI/UX parity; PR #358 stays Draft. Existing persisted old absolute paths are not migrated (fixed going forward on re-pick).
- **S4d-219b (this entry):** docs-only closeout — recorded the above in `progress.md`, `task_plan.md`, and the `docs/CONTEXT.md` Desktop-window row.

Its "avoid a default `REF_WIDTH` fix" recommendation is strengthened by S4d-212.

Device evidence slice; all export rows deleted, fixture retained; no repo/golden change.

## 2026-06-28 — S4d-191 MainViewModel business-IO lane accepted as NO-GO/defer

- **S4d-191 (read-only decision pack, accepted):** mapped the remaining `MainViewModel` / business-IO surface and found no safe non-speculative implementation slice in this lane right now. The cross-platform-consumed business logic is already extracted into commonMain and consumed off-Android (`WatermarkConfigEditor`, `OutputPrefsEditor`, `TemplateEditor`). The remaining pure-looking methods emit Android `UiState` only, and the rest is Android platform IO/render (`ContentResolver`, `MediaStore`, `Bitmap`/`Canvas`, `Compressor`, `FileProvider`, native `WatermarkRenderer` dispatch).
- **Rejected candidates:** a shared navigation/VM reducer would be consumerless; an IO `expect`/`actual` layer has no shared caller because Android `generateImage` cannot move while Android raster/composition stays native; promoting `DesktopSaveDecision.renderPlan` into commonMain and wiring Android through it is renderer-adjacent, semantically divergent (Android Image-mode guard is decode-failure, Desktop is empty-icon `require`), and low value.
- **Decision:** do not open a `MainViewModel` shared-VM/business-IO abstraction slice without a named real off-Android consumer or an explicit owner decision to fund a larger IO abstraction. Keep PR #358 Draft and keep 1:1 parity deferred.
- **Next:** resume from a separate release-migration lane with a real consumer/value signal, such as release safety-net/golden harness widening or remaining Desktop/iOS product/runtime gaps. Do not keep manufacturing tiny MainViewModel slices.

Skiko risk is low (`composeOverBackground` is android.graphics-backed on the android target; `:app` stays skiko-clean) — not the blocker.
- **`composeOverBackground` remains the Desktop/iOS composition path.** Reopening Android composition needs an explicit owner decision and must start with a **test-only Robolectric NATIVE FNV measurement** (compose both ways with the native cell `.asImageBitmap()`); no production routing without that gate.

## 2026-06-27 — S4d-116 iOS icon persistence accepted

- **S4d-116 (implementation, accepted; ACSP `20260627-165540...`; commit `676b741 Add iOS icon persistence`):** added iOS app-private icon-byte persistence (S4d-114 Option A). New `IosIconPersistence` writes non-empty picked icon bytes under `NSDocumentDirectory/watermark_icons/icon_<NSUUID>`, reads bytes back, and deletes replaced helper-owned files only after an ownership check. `IosWatermarkConfigBridge` now exposes `currentIconRef()`, `currentMarkMode()`, and `setIconFromBytes(bytes)`; the write order is file first, then `WatermarkConfigEditor.updateIcon(MediaRef(path))`, which flips `markMode` to Image, then best-effort cleanup of the prior owned file.

- **Safety revision:** coordinator requeued once because prefix-only ownership was too weak for `deleteIfOwned`. r1 hardened ownership to `prefix + non-empty filename suffix with no "/"`, so traversal/nested suffixes like `icon_/../../foreign` and `icon_x/foreign` are not owned and never deleted. `iconPersistence_ownership_rejects_traversal_and_nested_suffixes` proves the negative cases on the iOS runtime.

## 2026-06-27 — S4d-115 iOS icon render path accepted

- **S4d-115 (implementation, accepted; ACSP `20260627-163630...`; commit `d8e71bb Add iOS icon render path`):** added the Kotlin-only iOS icon render foundation in `IosWatermarkRenderer`: `renderIconCell(...)` wraps commonMain `WatermarkCellComposer.composeIconCell`, and `composeIconOverImage(...)` decodes background+icon bytes via `IosImageDecoder`, renders an icon cell, then composes through `composeOverBackground`.
- **Scope:** exactly two allowed files changed (`shared/src/iosMain/.../IosWatermarkRenderer.kt`, `shared/src/iosTest/.../IosWatermarkRendererTest.kt`), additive only. No Swift `WatermarkWorkflow`/`ContentView`, no `IosWatermarkRenderBridge`, no picker, no persistence/file IO, no `markMode` workflow, no Android/Desktop/commonMain signature/build/dependency/docs/golden changes.
- **Alpha/render boundary:** iOS renders the icon cell opaque and applies watermark alpha once at composition, mirroring the text `composeOverImage` path. This deliberately does not byte-match Android's native icon path, which bakes alpha into the cell and reuses the same alpha-bearing paint in `compose`; iOS icon output is perceptual Skiko honoring, not Android `Canvas.drawBitmap` byte parity.
- **Verification:** worker ran `:shared:iosSimulatorArm64Test` (new icon tests in `IosWatermarkRendererTest`, 7/0), strict Android goldens (`WATERMARK_GOLDEN_STRICT=true :app:testDebugUnitTest`, 51/0), and stopped Gradle daemons. Session moved to `done/`; bounded poll deleted.

## 2026-06-19 — S4d-21: Desktop EXIF orientation decode pass (accepted, in `done/`)

- **S4d-21 (ACSP `20260619-234634--s4d21-desktop-exif-orientation`):** `DesktopImageDecoder` now bakes JPEG **EXIF orientation** into the decoded `ImageBitmap` at the Desktop decode edge, matching the Android product policy (Android bakes EXIF via `androidx.exifinterface`/`BitmapUtils`). commonMain stays decode-free; Android untouched.
- **What landed (desktopMain):** a **tiny pure-JDK** EXIF reader in `DesktopImageDecoder` — `parseExifOrientation(bytes)` scans the JPEG APP1 `Exif\0\0` segment + reads TIFF IFD0 tag 0x0112 (II/MM byte order; best-effort → 1 on non-JPEG/missing/malformed), and `applyExifOrientation(BufferedImage, n)` rotates/flips via AWT `AffineTransformOp` (NEAREST, lossless) for all 8 orientations (5–8 swap w/h). `decode(bytes)`/`decode(file)` return an upright bitmap. **No new dependency** (no metadata library; pure `javax.imageio` + `java.awt`).
- **Test (desktopTest):** `DesktopExifOrientationTest` (7 tests) generates JPEGs deterministically (base image + a spliced minimal EXIF APP1, **no binary asset**) and asserts: tag parse for 1/3/6/8, default-1 for non-JPEG/missing/garbage, and that decode moves a bright top-left block to the expected corner with expected dims (1→TL same dims; 3→BR same dims; 6→TR swapped; 8→BL swapped), plus `composeOverRealImage` receives the oriented (swapped-dims) image.
- **Verification:** `git diff --check` clean; `:shared:desktopTest` **54/0** (incl. new 7); `:desktopApp:run` OK (PNG path unaffected — PNG has no EXIF → orientation 1); `:app:assembleDebug` UP-TO-DATE; `:app` + shared-android `debugRuntimeClasspath` show **no skiko/compose.desktop/imageio/metadata and no NEW dep** (the only `exif` match is the pre-existing `androidx.exifinterface` in `:app`, unchanged); daemon stopped. No commit/push.
- **Next:** S4d-20C — RUN the linked iOS test on an iOS runtime + `NSBundle` font loader; optionally extend Desktop EXIF to honour mirror cases in a real-photo fixture.

## 2026-06-19 — S4d-20B accepted: iOS renderer bring-up (text + composition + decode)

- **S4d-20B accepted (ACSP `20260619-232852--s4d20b-ios-renderer-bringup`):** brought the accepted commonMain renderer pipeline to the **iOS** target. `shared/src/iosMain` was empty; now has the iOS platform boundaries. Android production stays native.
- **What landed (iosMain):** `IosImageDecoder` (Skia `Image.makeFromEncoded` → `toComposeImageBitmap()`; throws on undecodable bytes) — the iOS analogue of Desktop `DesktopImageDecoder`; `IosTextRasterEnv` (`createFontFamilyResolver()` skiko resolver + `bundledFontFamily(latinBytes, cjkBytes, latinFirst)` via the skiko byte-`Font` factory — **no compose-resources**); `IosWatermarkRenderer` (renderTextCell + composeOverImage decode→cell→`composeOverBackground` + Skia PNG encode). Plus an `iosTest` `IosWatermarkRendererTest` (5 tests: env builds, Skia encode/decode round-trips dims, compose-over-decoded changes pixels, determinism, decoder rejects bad bytes).
- **Proof level:** both iOS targets **compile** (`compileKotlinIosSimulatorArm64` + `compileKotlinIosArm64`) and both iOS **test executables LINK** to native `.kexe` (`linkDebugTestIosSimulatorArm64` + `linkDebugTestIosArm64`) — the full renderer + decode + font boundary + test link end-to-end through all Skiko/Compose iOS APIs. The `iosSimulatorArm64Test` **run** is NOT possible here: `xcrun simctl list runtimes` is empty (no iOS runtime installed; running would trigger a multi-GB download — out of scope). Runtime execution deferred to an environment with an iOS runtime (or C5 iOS app).
- **Font-byte note:** iOS has no JVM-classpath resource loader and compose-resources is forbidden, so `bundledFontFamily` takes font **bytes as parameters** (a real iOS app supplies them from `NSBundle`/`NSData` at C5); the render proof uses `FontFamily.Default` (iOS system font). commonMain stays decode-free.
- **Verification:** `git diff --check` clean; iOS compile + test-link green; `:app:assembleDebug` UP-TO-DATE (iosMain change doesn't touch Android); `:app` + shared-android `debugRuntimeClasspath` 0 skiko / 0 compose.desktop / 0 ios / 0 FAILED; daemon stopped. No new dependency (Skia ships with the Compose iOS klibs). No commit/push.
- **Next:** S4d-20C/C5 — iOS app target (or a host-side iOS-runtime CI) to RUN `iosSimulatorArm64Test`, and an `NSBundle` font-bytes loader to feed `bundledFontFamily`; optionally a Desktop EXIF pass.

## 2026-06-19 — S4d-19 accepted: Desktop tiling/composition over a sample image

- **S4d-19 accepted (ACSP `20260618-232413--s4d19-desktop-tiling-composition`):** extended the Desktop renderer from one text cell (S4d-18) to a **full watermarked-image composition** over a generated sample background — REPEAT (tiled) and CLAMP (single decal) — the commonMain analogue of Android `WatermarkRenderer.compose`. Android composition stays native (untouched).
- **What landed:** new commonMain primitive `WatermarkCellComposer.composeOverBackground(background, cell, tileMode, offsetX, offsetY, alpha)` (platform-neutral Compose graphics: REPEAT grid-tiles the cell from origin; CLAMP draws one decal at the fractional offset). It explicitly supports **REPEAT/CLAMP only** and rejects MIRROR/DECAL, since those Android `BitmapShader` sampling modes are not reproduced by the origin-stepped draw loop. New desktopMain `DesktopWatermarkComposer` (deterministic asset-free generated sample background + Compose-free `ComposedImage` holder + AWT PNG encode). `:desktopApp:run` now also writes `build/s4d19-desktop-watermark/{repeat_watermark,clamp_watermark}.png` (640×480). New Desktop gate `DesktopWatermarkCompositionGoldenTest` (6 tests: dims==bg, REPEAT spreads ink over ≥2 rows/cols, CLAMP localized < REPEAT, determinism, PNG magic, unsupported MIRROR/DECAL rejected).
- **Visual check:** both PNGs viewed — REPEAT shows the watermark grid-tiled (rotated 315° 请勿转载 / DO NOT REDISTRIBUTE) over a dark striped background; CLAMP shows a single localized decal. Real CJK+Latin glyphs.
- **Verification:** coordinator reran `git diff --check`, `:shared:desktopTest --rerun-tasks` (42/0; composition gate 6/0), `:desktopApp:run` (7 PNGs total), `:app:assembleDebug`, strict `:app:cleanTestDebugUnitTest :app:testDebugUnitTest --rerun-tasks`, and `:app` + shared-android `debugRuntimeClasspath` leakage checks (**0 skiko / 0 compose.desktop / 0 FAILED**); both daemons stopped. No commit/push.
- **Next:** S4d-20 should take the smaller realistic Desktop route first: add Desktop real-image decode via AWT/ImageIO and compose the existing S4d-19 watermark over an actual decoded image. iOS text/composition bring-up remains the next platform route after that.

## 2026-06-18 — S4d-17 accepted: Android text stays native; Desktop/iOS text first

- **S4d-17 accepted:** decision pack `20260618-215837--s4d17-line-metric-cjk-threshold` is complete and moved to ACSP `done/`. It was read-only and introduced no repo code changes.
- **Decision:** adopt Option C. Android watermark text remains native indefinitely (`WatermarkRenderer.buildTextShader` / `StaticLayout`); bundled commonMain `composeTextCell` is the Desktop/iOS text renderer first. Android CJK parity remains log-only unless the owner explicitly reopens a draw-swap with a signed perceptual tolerance and golden rebaseline.
- **Evidence basis:** S4d-16 corrected data showed Latin/bold byte-identical, italic/bold-italic within the perceptual gate, and CJK still at `IoU 0.716/0.691` with the same bundled fonts on both sides. The remaining CJK gap is therefore an engine difference (`StaticLayout` vs `MultiParagraph`), not a font problem.
- **Next:** S4d-18 should wire bundled `composeTextCell` into the Desktop surface and add a Desktop perceptual text golden, without touching Android production text or Android goldens.

## 2026-06-18 — S4d-16 accepted: test-only bundled font env and corrected parity gate

- **S4d-16 accepted:** exact Noto Sans Latin regular + Noto Sans CJK SC regular artifacts were added as test-only desktop/android resources behind `TextRasterEnv`; Android production text rendering stays native (`WatermarkRenderer.buildTextShader`), with no production loader, no compose-resources, no golden rebaseline, no commit/push.
- **Coordinator re-review corrected the measurement:** round 2 had a P1 comparator bug where `androidBundledCell` dropped the requested `TextTypeface` style; round 3 fixed it with `Typeface.create(bundledTypeface(latinFirst), cfg.textTypeface.obtainSysTypeface())`, corrected `colorDiff` denominators, and removed stale CJK-first "~2x" risk wording.
- **Final evidence:** coordinator reran `git diff --check` and `ANDROID_SERIAL=emulator-5554 ./gradlew :app:connectedDebugAndroidTest -Pandroid.testInstrumentationRunnerArguments.class=me.rosuh.easywatermark.render.WatermarkTextRasterParityInstrumentedTest --max-workers=8 --console=plain`; focused gate passed 2 tests on Pixel_9_Pro_XL emulator / API 36, then Gradle daemon was stopped.
- **Parity conclusion:** Latin and bold are byte-identical with the bundle; italic/bold-italic are near (`IoU 0.921/0.934`, within the existing perceptual class); CJK remains the only large text raster gap (`IoU 0.716/0.691`) and is engine-driven (`StaticLayout` vs `MultiParagraph`), not solved by the font.
- **Next:** completed by S4d-17; the accepted path is Android-native text plus Desktop/iOS commonMain text first.

## 2026-06-18 — S4d-15 accepted: bundled-font strategy fixed to Option A

- **S4d-15 accepted:** ADR-0010 now records owner Option A for future cross-platform watermark text: bundle a Latin + CJK family (`Noto Sans` + `Noto Sans CJK SC` class, OFL-compatible) and delegate emoji to platform fallback. This is a strategy/parity-gate decision, not an Android text draw-swap.
- **Evidence captured:** after S4d-14C, single-line Latin in all four styles is byte-identical; Latin rotated, multiline, and emoji are near; CJK remains the large gap (`IoU 0.39-0.70`, `colorDiff 12.6%-46.3%`) and stays LOG-ONLY until a bundled-font measurement plus owner-approved threshold.
- **Scope respected:** S4d-15 changed docs/test-only parity corpus rows and ACSP artifacts; it did not add font binaries, alter build/resource wiring, touch Android production text wiring, rebaseline goldens, commit, or push.
- **Next:** S4d-16 adds exact Latin+CJK font artifacts behind `TextRasterEnv`, records license/size/APK delta, re-measures CJK on device, and still leaves Android production `WatermarkRenderer.buildTextShader` native.

## 2026-06-18 — S4d-14C accepted: Android multiline text vertical centring fixed

- **S4d-14C (owner Option C) accepted:** Android production `WatermarkRenderer.buildTextShader` now centres the full `StaticLayout` block with `(finalHeight - staticLayout.height) / 2` instead of using only line 0. This fixes the historical multiline bottom clipping while keeping Android text rendering native; no commonMain text draw-swap occurred.
- **Golden impact is multiline-only:** `WatermarkExportGoldenTest` and `WatermarkExportInstrumentedGoldenTest` rebaselined exactly two rows each (`multiline`, `cjk_multiline_315`) under the owner-approved Option C. Single-line/icon rows were byte-identical.
- **Verification accepted:** strict unit goldens passed 41/0 after rebaseline; instrumented export + raster + multiline probe passed 6/0 on `emulator-5554` / API 36. Probe evidence: multiline IoU 0.135→0.984, Android opaque pixels 1635→2167, `androidBottomRowOpaque` 89→0, bbox matching commonMain after the fix. `assembleDebug` passed, `git diff --check` clean, Gradle daemon stopped.
- **Screenshot caveat accepted:** the matched production-vs-debug multiline screenshot was not completed because production v2.10.0 uses the legacy pre-S3/S4 View pipeline and is not a clean before/after for this branch renderer fix. The coordinator accepted the device-pixel golden/probe evidence plus a fixed debug screenshot; a manual visual confirm remains optional before landing.
- **Next:** choose a post-cutoff slice rather than auto-starting more work. The likely routes are bundled-font ADR + on-device text raster parity, or Desktop/iOS renderer consumption where `composeTextCell`/`composeIconCell` are used directly without Android byte-exact constraints.

## 2026-06-18 — S4d-6/S4d-7 blocked; S4d-8 applies Option A (Android icon stays native)

- **S4d-6 (icon draw-swap candidate, blocked):** wired Android `WatermarkRenderer.buildIconShader` to delegate the offscreen icon cell to commonMain `composeIconCell`. Byte-identical for solid/axis-aligned icons, but strict `WatermarkExportGoldenTest.icon_rot_315` (non-uniform icon @ 315° = production default) failed by 16/1936 colour px. Blocked, no rebaseline.
- **S4d-7 (float-precision fix, blocked):** changed `composeIconCell` to Android-float pivot + float top-left point-draw (pre-scale intermediate + `drawImage(topLeft)`). Correct for placement; delta improved 16→5 px; still not byte-exact. Root cause is a commonMain limitation, not placement: no `drawImage` overload offers float-placement AND nearest-filter together (`dstSize` = nearest but rect-map half-texel; `topLeft` = correct placement but bilinear). `nativeCanvas.drawBitmap` (the only API with both) is platform-specific, not commonMain. Blocked, no rebaseline.
- **S4d-8 (strategy cleanup, Option A — this slice):** coordinator chose Option A. Reverted the two `:app` files (native `buildIconShader` restored; the swap-tied frozen-legacy 315 hard gate removed by reverting the parity test to its S4d-5 pre-swap form). **Kept** the `composeIconCell` float-placement improvement (strictly more correct; Desktop/iOS icon renderer). Full strict gate is GREEN **without rebaseline**: `:app:testDebugUnitTest` 41/0 (incl. `WatermarkExportGoldenTest` 5/0 with `strict=true`, `WatermarkRendererCommonParityTest` 6/0), `:shared` desktop+iOS compile + `WatermarkIconCellRasterTest` 7/0; `assembleDebug` OK; deps clean (0 FAILED/skiko/compose.desktop). Only dirty file after cleanup: `WatermarkCellComposer.kt`.
- **Durable rule recorded:** ADR-0004 addendum + AGENTS.md (state bullet + conventions guard) + this log + `findings.md`. Android icon production stays native; commonMain icon raster targets Desktop/iOS; do not re-attempt the byte-exact Android icon swap without an owner decision (tolerance golden policy or commonMain nearest blitter).
- **Next:** bundled-font ADR + on-device text raster parity (text draw-swap prerequisite), and/or Desktop/iOS bring-up consuming `composeIconCell`/`composeTextCell`.

## 2026-05-19 (cont.) — State promotion to ViewModel (milestone A) completed

- `MainViewModel`: split into `private var _userPreferences` + public read-only `val userPreferences: StateFlow<UserPreferences> = _userPreferences`.
- `saveOutput(format = current, level = current)` given defaults pulling current persisted values, so partial updates work via named args (`saveOutput(newFormat)` / `saveOutput(level = q)`). Partial-update semantics now owned by the ViewModel, not the call site.

- Verified `./gradlew app:assembleDebug` succeeds; behavior self-test passed including persistence across full app restart.

Fixed a Koin double-start across the suite (`@Config(application=Application::class)`) and wired `:app:testDebugUnitTest` into the PR check (`e09ff00`). No emulator/instrumented load (host-resource discipline).
- **C2a engine-wiring attempt → REVERTED (important learning).** Tried delegating `buildTextBitmapShader`'s cell sizing (gap helpers + rotated-AABB) to commonMain `WatermarkGeometry` — formulas character-identical, the dimension golden stayed 93×33, and there was NO logcat crash. **The wired build's editor showed a ~53KB (mostly-dark) screen** while a pre-wiring build showed 2.3MB (image). I initially read this as "wiring broke the preview" and reverted — but later found this evidence CONFOUNDED (see the corrected verdict below: ~60KB is ambiguous between blank-editor and LaunchScreen, and the share-in test harness is flaky). Reverted to protect the product core regardless; committed state renders correctly via normal use.
  - **Finding:** a **dimension-only** golden is NOT sufficient to gate C2a engine wiring — the regression was invisible to cell-size assertions and to logcat. **C1.7 must capture actual RENDERED output** (pixel/image comparison of the composed preview/export, e.g. Robolectric NATIVE bitmap → PNG baseline), not just cell dimensions, before any C2a delegation is safe. Root cause of the blank preview is not yet diagnosed (no exception surfaced) — a focused C2a session needs the pixel golden first, then bisect the delegation.
  - This concretely validates the plan's golden-FIRST gate (D4/F1/F16) and the product-core caution.
- **Pixel golden — both JVM (Robolectric) + instrumented (device), CORRECTED methodology (`efa722b`→`7f0da28`).** Upgraded the golden to pixel-level (composite the tiled cell, assert non-blank rendered pixels — catches the blank-render class the dimension golden missed). **Self-corrected a wrong intermediate finding:** I briefly concluded "Robolectric can't render emoji+rotation" (emoji@315° showed 0 px) — that was MY TEST BUG: a fixed 64/96px sample window landed on a transparent CORNER of the large rotated emoji cell. Fixed by sizing the sample to the cell's own dimensions. Corrected result: emoji "👋 DO NOT REDISTRIBUTE"@315° renders non-blank in **both** Robolectric NATIVE (2755 px) **and** on-device instrumented (2764 px); ascii@0 matches JVM↔device exactly (845 px). ⇒ **Robolectric IS a usable cheap CI golden oracle** for these configs; the new `WatermarkCellInstrumentedGoldenTest` (androidTest, verified green on S22+) adds the device-faithful tier. Lesson: verify the test harness itself (sampling) before trusting a golden's verdict.
- **C2a RESOLVED → DONE & verified (`9f9eb6e`). The "wiring broke the preview" was a FALSE ALARM — root cause was a lost media permission.** With reliable editor entry (Choose Images → in-app GalleryDialog) I found a runtime-permission dialog: the app had **lost `READ_MEDIA_IMAGES`** (from the reinstall cycles), so share-in silently fell back to LaunchScreen and a ~60KB screencap (LaunchScreen, NOT a blank editor) read as "blank". After `pm grant READ_MEDIA_IMAGES`, BOTH the committed and the **re-applied wired** build render the watermark **identically** on-device (preview min/max/stddev 0/227/46.8 identical; soccer photo with gold 315° tiling; no exception). ⇒ C2a wiring is correct; **re-applied and committed** — the live Android renderer now delegates cell sizing to commonMain `WatermarkGeometry`. **Lessons compounded:** (1) view the screenshot, never infer from size; (2) rule out the test harness / environment (here: a revoked permission) before blaming the code; (3) my two-revert caution was right to protect the product core, but the diagnosis only converged once I used a reliable entry path.
- (superseded) earlier intermediate note read: ~~"C2a verdict UNDETERMINED — confounded"~~ — On deeper device testing I found my evidence was ambiguous: a ~60KB screencap is indistinguishable between a *blank editor* and the *LaunchScreen* (both mostly-dark), and the `am start ACTION_SEND --eu` test method is currently **not reliably navigating to the editor** at all — cold-start shares now consistently land on the LaunchScreen (share-in/URI-grant flakiness in this test harness, even with `--grant-read-uri-permission`), independent of the code. So the wired "53KB blank" may simply have been a flaky share-in→LaunchScreen, NOT a render break. What IS solid: the cell-output goldens show the wired `buildTextBitmapShader` is output-identical (dims 93×33 + non-blank incl. emoji@315) and throws no exception. **Net: whether C2a wiring affects the live preview is UNDETERMINED.** Kept it REVERTED (safe default — don't ship unverified product-core change). Re-verification needs **reliable editor entry** (picker-based, not `am start` share-in) + view-the-screenshot (not size) before deciding. Lesson: don't conclude from screencap *size*; view it, and rule out the test harness before blaming the code.

- **CMP C1.2 — typed `@Serializable` routes (`f5e9038`).** Added kotlinx.serialization (plugin via root `apply false` + json dep) and `ui/Routes.kt` (Launch/GalleryDialog/Editor/About/OpenSource objects); NavHost → `composable<T>`/`dialog<T>`/`navigate(route)`/`popUpTo(route)`. Real-device verified: Launch→About→OpenSource, back-stack pops, share-in→Editor — no serialization/NavType crashes. Removes string-route fragility + Parcelize-from-common blocker (D3).
- **CMP D7 (partial) — platform-neutral `ImageFormat` (`d07e03d`).** Removed `android.graphics.Bitmap.CompressFormat` from the model layer (`UserPreferences`/`UserConfigRepository`/`MainViewModel`/`SaveExportSheet`) → app-owned `data.model.ImageFormat { JPEG, PNG }`; platform encoder mapped only at the edge (`utils/ktx/ImageFormatExt.toCompressFormat()`). Backward-compatible persistence via `storageId` == historical ordinals (no DataStore migration; R6). Real-device verified: JPEG/PNG render, PNG hides quality slider, write→read `storageId` round-trip persists. **TileMode deferred** — it feeds the live `BitmapShader` render path, gated behind the golden harness (C2a).
- **Honest status:** the next CMP steps are large/risky and the plan gates the engine work behind a golden harness (C1.7) that does not yet exist. Sequence from here: C1.1 state consolidation (kill LiveData in MainViewModel — CMP-required, no golden needed) → C1.7 golden harness → C2a/C2b engine → C3 deps (incl. TileMode) → C4 `:shared`+Desktop → C5 iOS → C6. This is months of part-time work; not completable in one session. No fabrication of completion.

## 2026-06-17 — S4d-3 commonMain text raster bootstrap accepted

- S4d-3 ACSP session `20260617-075849--s4d3-text-raster-bootstrap` was accepted and moved to `done/`.
- Accepted route: add `TextRasterEnv` and `WatermarkTextContent` to `:shared/commonMain`, then add `WatermarkCellComposer.composeTextCell` as a proofed text-raster primitive. The platform font resolver is injected at the boundary instead of constructed in commonMain.
- Placement correction from review: `MultiParagraph.paint` paints from the current canvas origin, so commonMain centres the measured text box by translating to `((finalWidth - textWidth)/2, (finalHeight - textHeight)/2)`. Android's `translate(finalW/2, ...)` remains correct only because the Android path uses CENTER-aligned `TextPaint`; the parity artifact now explicitly documents this distinction.
- Verification: worker ran shared desktop/iOS compile, `:shared:desktopTest`, strict renderer goldens, app dependency proof, and `git diff --check`; coordinator re-ran `./gradlew :shared:desktopTest --max-workers=8` and `git diff --check`, then stopped Gradle daemons.
- Production preview/export were not wired to the new text primitive. Android rendering remains on `WatermarkRenderer`; no `:app` files changed, no commit/push/golden rebaseline happened inside the worker session.
- Next step: S4d-4 should add icon raster / image decode bootstrap for the commonMain cell artifact, still without production wiring. Before any draw-swap, require bundled-font strategy, Android-vs-commonMain pixel gates, strict goldens, and production-first paired screenshots.

## 2026-06-17 — S4d-4 commonMain icon raster bootstrap accepted

- S4d-4 ACSP session `20260617-090920--s4d4-icon-raster-bootstrap` was accepted and moved to `done/` after one coordinator revision.
- Accepted route: add `WatermarkCellComposer.composeIconCell` to `:shared/commonMain`. The primitive accepts an already-decoded Compose `ImageBitmap`, keeps decode/EXIF/downsample/recycled-bitmap checks outside commonMain, uses the Android-equivalent icon scale reference (`textSize / 14f`), sizes through `WatermarkGeometry.diagonal` + gaps, rotates around the cell centre, centres the scaled image, uses `FilterQuality.None`, and applies normalized/clamped alpha.
- Review correction: the first worker result missed the image watermark alpha contract. The revision added `alpha: Float = 1f`, clamps it to `0f..1f`, passes it to `drawImage(alpha = ...)`, and added tests that fail for a full-opacity-only implementation. Coordinator also corrected stale artifact wording: current production default `WaterMark.alpha` is `255`; `128` is only the nullable-config `Paint.applyConfig` fallback.
- Verification: shared desktop/iOS compile, `:shared:desktopTest`, strict renderer golden gate, dependency proof, and `git diff --check` all passed; Gradle daemon was stopped. `WatermarkIconCellRasterTest` now covers visible pixels, geometry/scale, gap behavior, rotation, centring, alpha scaling, and alpha clamping.
- Production preview/export were not wired to the new icon primitive. Android rendering remains on `WatermarkRenderer`; no `:app` files changed, no commit/push/golden rebaseline happened inside the worker session.
- Next step: S4d-5 should add the first Android-vs-commonMain parity gate before any draw-swap. Keep it test-only unless the parity evidence is strong: text/icon cell pixels, bundled-font decision for CJK/emoji, strict goldens, and production-first screenshots when UI is touched.

## 2026-06-17 — S4d-5 Android-vs-commonMain parity gate accepted

- S4d-5 ACSP session `20260617-231553--s4d5-common-renderer-parity-gate` was accepted and moved to `done/`.
- Accepted route: add one test-only JVM/Robolectric NATIVE gate, `WatermarkRendererCommonParityTest`, comparing Android `WatermarkRenderer` against commonMain `WatermarkCellComposer` through the same Android graphics backend. No production code, commonMain code, build files, or golden baselines changed.
- Icon cell gate is hard enough for the next decision: exact dimensions across 18 degree/gap/scale cases, alpha parity exact in the tested full/half-opacity cases, opaque-footprint IoU measured at 1.0 for degree 0/45/90 with a gate floor of 0.95, and centring structure on both paths.
- Text cell gate is deliberately limited: exact cell dimensions for simple ASCII and nonblank ASCII @0. It does not claim text raster parity for `StaticLayout` vs `MultiParagraph`, CJK, emoji, or rotated text. That remains a bundled-font + on-device gate.
- Coordinator-side verification reran the new parity test, full strict unit gate with `:shared:desktopTest`, dependency proof, `git diff --check`, and stopped Gradle. JUnit XML confirmed `WatermarkRendererCommonParityTest` 6/0, `WatermarkCellGoldenTest` 4/0, `WatermarkExportGoldenTest` 5/0 with `strict=true`, `WatermarkIconCellRasterTest` 7/0, and `WatermarkTextCellRasterTest` 4/0.
- Next step: decide S4d-6. The lower-risk product-facing path is likely icon-first draw-swap candidate, but it should still add on-device/real-icon parity before any production flip. Text draw-swap should wait for bundled-font ADR and on-device raster parity.

## 2026-06-16 — S4c accepted; S4d-1 spike blocked as C4.3 lineage work

- S4c C4/CMP-9547/Compose-lineage gate completed and accepted. Conclusion: commonMain Compose graphics/text should only proceed through a dependency topology spike, not a renderer rewrite.
- S4d-1 topology spike ran and was accepted as a valid blocker. The preferred Shape B2 (`androidx.compose.*:1.10.6` in `commonMain`, no `org.jetbrains.compose` plugin) compiled Android + desktop but failed iOS because `ui-text:1.10.6` has no iOS klib.
- Shape B1 (`org.jetbrains.compose` 1.11.1) proved the commonMain Compose types can compile on Android + desktop + iOS and kept `:app:assembleDebug`, unit tests, and forced strict renderer golden green. However it forced app Compose `1.10.6 -> 1.11.2`, introduced `org.jetbrains.compose.*` nodes into `:app` runtime, and left `foundation` at 1.10.6 while `ui/runtime` moved to 1.11.2.
- Decision: do **not** land the S4d-1 patch. Treat this as C4.3 Compose-lineage unification work. The next task is a dedicated single-lineage migration plan/candidate that either moves `:app` + `:shared` coherently onto one CMP/Compose line with full UI/golden verification, or identifies a lower-risk no-bump pin if one exists.

## 2026-06-21 — S4d-23 iOS EXIF orientation boundary (accepted, in `done/`; finding: Skia bakes it)

- ACSP session `20260620-234014--s4d23-ios-exif-orientation-boundary` claimed, completed, coordinator-accepted, and moved to `done/`.
- **Finding:** skiko's `Image.makeFromEncoded().toComposeImageBitmap()` (the exact `IosImageDecoder` path) ALREADY bakes EXIF orientation — proven at runtime on the desktop skiko proxy (`SkiaExifDecodeProbeTest`: orientation-6 → upright 16×24/TR; 8 → BL; no-EXIF → 24×16/TL). Contrast: AWT `ImageIO` (Desktop) and `BitmapFactory` (Android) do NOT bake; Skia (iOS) does.
- **Decision:** iOS needs NO orientation transform; `IosImageDecoder` stays pure-Skia (S4d-20B, code byte-identical). Applying one would double-rotate. A speculative commonMain `ExifOrientation.parse`+`apply` was built then DELETED after the desktop proxy proved Skia bakes — the evidence-first process caught a double-rotation bug before any production wiring.
- New: `SkiaExifDecodeProbeTest` (desktopTest, RUNS — proves + regression-guards Skia baking) + `IosExifOrientationTest` (iosTest, compile/link-proven; RUN deferred to S4d-20C *— since RUN and passed on the iOS 27.0 simulator in S4d-55, 2026-06-26*). `IosImageDecoder` KDoc-only update; `DesktopImageDecoder` net-unchanged from S4d-21.
- Net production behavior change: NONE. No new dep, no compose-resources, no binary fixture, no golden rebaseline, commonMain decode-free (no commonMain code added), Android untouched, no commit/push.
- Verification: `git diff --check` clean; `:shared:desktopTest` 62/0 (SkiaExifDecodeProbeTest 3/0, DesktopExifOrientationTest 12/0 regression intact); iOS both targets compile + both test executables LINK; daemon stopped. `:app:assembleDebug` skipped (no Android/non-test production change).
- Next: S4d-20C — RUN `iosSimulatorArm64Test` (incl. `IosExifOrientationTest`) on an iOS runtime + NSBundle font loader; reintroduce `ExifOrientation.apply` only if the iOS run shows skiko does NOT bake. *(Update — S4d-55, 2026-06-26: this RUN happened on the iOS 27.0 simulator; `IosExifOrientationTest` passed → skiko bakes EXIF on Native, so `ExifOrientation.apply` was NOT reintroduced.)*

## 2026-06-24 — S4d-49 MediaRef readiness + S4d-50 MediaRef iconUri (accepted, in `done/`)

- **S4d-49 (read-only decision pack, ACSP `20260623-221822...`):** mapped every `android.net.Uri` surface in the worktree. Domain/model leaks: `WaterMark.iconUri` (the **only** persisted Uri — via `KEY_ICON_URI`, already string-backed `stringPreferencesKey`), `ImageInfo.uri`/`shareUri` (transient, not persisted), and `WaterMarkRepository.imageInfoMap<Uri, Int>`. `KEY_URI`/`SP_KEY_URI` is dead (declared, never read/written). Everything else is Android-edge IO/UI (picker contracts, `BitmapUtils`, `FileUtils`, MediaStore/FileProvider). Recommended first slice = flip only `WaterMark.iconUri` (zero migration, bounded call graph); `ImageInfo.uri` + picker/share/export are later slices.
- **S4d-50 (Android + shared/commonMain, ACSP `20260623-230516...`):** landed the first real platform-neutral `MediaRef` and flipped `WaterMark.iconUri: android.net.Uri → MediaRef`. Accepted after two coordinator revisions:
  - `MediaRef` = **`@JvmInline value class MediaRef(val value: String)`** in `shared/commonMain/data/model` with **`import kotlin.jvm.JvmInline`**. The v1 submission omitted the import, misdiagnosed the resulting iOS compile failure as a KMP limitation, and switched to a `data class` fallback — the coordinator correctly rejected that premise; v2 added the import and restored `@JvmInline value class`, green on all 3 targets. **Durable lesson: commonMain value classes need `import kotlin.jvm.JvmInline`; the annotation is not auto-imported.**
  - Android edge: `utils/ktx/MediaRefExt.kt` (`MediaRef.toUri()` / `Uri.toMediaRef()`), mirroring the `ImageFormat`/`WatermarkTileMode` mapper seam. `WaterMark.iconUri: MediaRef`; repo read `MediaRef.parse(it[KEY_ICON_URI] ?: "")`, write `iconUri.value`; `MainViewModel`/`EditorScreen` decode via `.toUri()`; `IconOption` picker returns `Uri` → `it.toMediaRef()` at the edge (callback `(FuncTitleModel, MediaRef)`); `ColorStyleOption` `@Preview` fixture `MediaRef.Empty`. 10 mechanical test-fixture edits (`Uri.EMPTY → MediaRef.Empty`).
  - **Storage byte-identical** under `KEY_ICON_URI` (no migration); **strict goldens byte-identical** (no rebaseline) — `MediaRef(s).toUri() == Uri.parse(s)` by construction, so icon decode/export pixels are unchanged.
  - Verified: `:shared:compileKotlin{Desktop,IosSimulatorArm64,IosArm64}`, `:shared:desktopTest`, `WATERMARK_GOLDEN_STRICT=true :app:testDebugUnitTest --rerun-tasks`, `:app:assembleDebug`+`assembleRelease` (R8) all green; 20 files (4 new + 16 edited); HEAD `ef1da72` unchanged, no commit/push.
  - `ImageInfo.uri`/`shareUri`, picker contracts, `imageInfoMap`, and save/share/export Uri surfaces are **still `android.net.Uri`** (out of scope).
- **S4d-51 (read-only readiness/decision pack, ACCEPTED 2026-06-24, ACSP `20260624-091450...` in `done/` after a r2 coordinator correction):** mapped the full `ImageInfo.uri`/`shareUri`/`imageInfoMap<Uri,Int>` call graph. `ImageInfo.uri` is **transient** (never persisted — `KEY_URI`/`SP_KEY_URI` dead; lives in in-memory `_imageMapFlow`/`_selectedImage`, rebuilt each session) → **zero migration**, unlike `iconUri`. **7 constructor sites** feed a `Uri` into `ImageInfo`. **Corrected r2 fact:** the system-picker reducer site `MainViewModel.kt:1011-1012` is **`ImageInfo(Uri)`**, not `ImageInfo(Image)` — `Action.SystemPickerImageSelected` declares `val uriList: List<Uri>` (`LaunchScreen.kt:170-172`) and `newList = action.uriList`, so `it: Uri` via `ImageInfo`'s only (`Uri`) primary ctor; there is **no latent compile issue** (the r1 draft's warning was false and was removed before acceptance). `imageInfoMap<Uri,Int>` keys on `imageInfo.uri` so it flips to `MediaRef` automatically (string equality); `ImageInfo.shareUri` (`result?.data as? Uri?`) appears **unused** in the Compose build (possibly dead). No repo edits; HEAD `ef1da72` unchanged.
- **C3 ImageInfo.uri → MediaRef (S4d-52, Android, ACCEPTED 2026-06-25):** implemented Option 1 — flipped `ImageInfo.uri: Uri → MediaRef` and `WaterMarkRepository.imageInfoMap` to `MediaRef` keying, confining `Uri` to the Android edges. 5 files (`ImageInfo.kt`, `WaterMarkRepository.kt`, `MainViewModel.kt`, `EditorScreen.kt`, `ComposeMainActivity.kt`). `Uri→MediaRef` at picker reducer / share-in (`generateImageInfoList`) / gallery (`selectGallery`/`onGalleryDismiss`) / `buildPreviewShader` / compress-output via `it.toMediaRef()`; `MediaRef→Uri` at export+preview decode, compress `openInputStream`, filmstrip + save-sheet Coil via `.uri.toUri()` (`MediaRefExt`). `MediaRef` value-class string equality preserves `imageInfoMap`/`isSameItem`/`select` semantics. **Kept `Uri` (Android edges):** `shareUri` (`Uri?`, deferred/possibly dead), gallery `Image.uri`, `Action.SystemPickerImageSelected.uriList`, `SaveExportSheet.imageUris`, picker contracts, `BitmapUtils`/`BitmapCache`/`FileUtils`. No persistence change (transient; `KEY_URI`/`SP_KEY_URI` retained). Verified: `git diff --check` clean; `:app:compileDebugKotlin`; `WATERMARK_GOLDEN_STRICT=true` unit gate **byte-identical, no rebaseline** (`MediaRef(s).toUri()==Uri.parse(s)`); `:app:assembleDebug`+`assembleRelease` (R8); daemon stopped. **Device gate on `emulator-5554`** (fresh candidate APK, `READ_MEDIA_IMAGES` granted): **5 flows pass** — picker→editor, editor preview renders, filmstrip thumbnails, save/export-sheet thumbnails, ACTION_SEND share-in lands+renders; no `NoBeanDef`/MediaRef-ClassCast/FATAL (one benign system `Bundle` ClassCast warning on single-Uri ACTION_SEND, pre-existing, not a regression). Accepted after one r1 (removed a duplicate `toUri` import). No full-page pixel-parity claim vs the production View stack. HEAD `ef1da72` unchanged, no commit/push. **Next future slices** *(both since DONE: `shareUri` removed in S4d-53, dead `KEY_URI`/`SP_KEY_URI` removed in S4d-54 — see below)*: `shareUri` liveness/removal; dead `KEY_URI` deletion.

Liveness: across `app/src`+`shared/src` the only `shareUri` occurrences were the property definition + a comment — **no `.shareUri` access, no `::shareUri`/`"shareUri"` reflection, no test/androidTest reference**; the Compose share-out button is an unwired empty lambda (`onShareClick = {}` @ `ComposeMainActivity:426`). The real export result is `ImageInfo.result`/`jobState` (set by `MainViewModel.generateImage`), untouched — removing the computed `shareUri` (`result?.data as? Uri?`) drops no data. Edit: 1 file (`ImageInfo.kt`) — removed the `shareUri` property + the now-unused `import android.net.Uri`. Verified: `git diff --check` clean; `:app:compileDebugKotlin`+`:app:testDebugUnitTest` green; daemon stopped; HEAD `ef1da72` unchanged, no commit/push. Docs updated to record `shareUri` removed (AGENTS/CONTEXT/ADR-0007 + task_plan/findings). Did **not** delete `KEY_URI`/`SP_KEY_URI` (separate deferred slice) or touch any forbidden edge. **Only remaining model-`Uri` hygiene** *(since DONE in S4d-54 below)*: dead `KEY_URI`/`SP_KEY_URI` deletion.
- **C3 KEY_URI cleanup (S4d-54, Android, in review):** removed the dead `KEY_URI`/`SP_KEY_URI` DataStore key declarations (2 lines in `WaterMarkRepository.kt`) — the last model-`Uri` hygiene item. Liveness re-proven: the only source occurrences were the two declarations (`val KEY_URI = stringPreferencesKey(SP_KEY_URI)` @ 48, `const val SP_KEY_URI = "${SP_NAME}_key_uri"` @ 209); **no `it[KEY_URI]` read/write** anywhere, no other `SP_KEY_URI`/`sp_water_mark…key_uri` user. `KEY_ICON_URI` (live) + all real watermark prefs untouched; **no persisted behavior change, no DataStore migration**. Verified: `git diff --check` clean; `:app:compileDebugKotlin`+`:app:testDebugUnitTest` green; daemon stopped; HEAD `ef1da72` unchanged, no commit/push. Docs updated (AGENTS/CONTEXT/ADR-0007 + task_plan/findings) — **no model-layer `Uri` hygiene remains**. **The platform-neutral model layer is now `Uri`-free** (C3 model de-Androidization `TileMode`/`ImageFormat`/`MediaRef` complete); remaining `Uri` is all genuine Android edges. ## 2026-06-26 — S4d-55 iOS runtime proof (accepted, in `done/`); S4d-55b docs closeout

- **S4d-55 (C5.3 runtime proof, accepted, ACSP `20260626-080649...` in `done/`):** the previously runtime-deferred iOS gate is now proven on the installed **iOS 27.0** simulator (`com.apple.CoreSimulator.SimRuntime.iOS-27-0`, Xcode 27.0).
  - **Shared iOS suite RAN** — `./gradlew :shared:iosSimulatorArm64Test --max-workers=8` → BUILD SUCCESSFUL, **41/41 tests pass** across 10 classes (was compile/link-only). The 5 iOS-specific suites all ran: `IosFontLoaderTest` 3/3, `IosExifOrientationTest` 2/2, `IosWatermarkRendererTest` 5/5 (full decode→render→compose→encode pipeline), `IosWatermarkRenderBridgeTest` 5/5, `IosByteArrayInteropTest` 4/4.
  - **EXIF on Native confirmed** — `IosExifOrientationTest` passed, proving skiko bakes EXIF orientation on iOS at runtime (the S4d-23 decision, previously only desktop-proxy-verified). **No `IosImageDecoder` orientation fix was needed** — the conditional repair did NOT trigger. **No source code changed in S4d-55.**
  - **iosApp packaged, launched, ran shared code live** — `xcodebuild` (simulator SDK) BUILD SUCCEEDED with `Shared.framework` embedded + both Noto faces (`NotoSans-Regular.ttf`/`NotoSansSC-Regular.otf`) as Copy Bundle Resources; installed + launched on `iPhone 17 Pro` (no crash); the launch screenshot shows the on-screen witness `:shared linked ✓  geometry.diagonal(100×100) = 141` → commonMain `WatermarkGeometry` executing through the embedded Kotlin/Native framework on the runtime.
  - **Still NOT proven** *(as of S4d-55; SUPERSEDED below — render+export later proven via the S4d-58 fixture seam; only real PHPicker cell-selection remains)*: the full `PhotosPicker → render → Share/Save` UI flow. Blocked only by missing UI-automation tooling (no `idb`/`cliclick`/XCUITest; `PHPickerViewController` is out-of-process) — an environment gap, NOT a product failure; the render engine behind the button is independently runtime-proven by the shared suite. Next slice = S4d-56 on a machine with `idb`/XCUITest. **No overclaim of full iOS product-flow completion.**
- **S4d-55b (docs closeout, THIS slice, docs-only, ACSP `20260626-082217...`):** folded the accepted S4d-55 facts into the durable docs — `AGENTS.md`, `task_plan.md` (J.20 → `[x]`; C5.3 → shared-tests/launch done, picker flow open), `progress.md` (this entry + corrected the stale "C5.3 still deferred / runtimes empty" line and the historical S4d-20C "Next" pointers), `findings.md`. No source/build/test/golden/iOS-project edits; `docs/CONTEXT.md` and ADR-0007 needed no change. `git diff --check` clean; no commit/push.

## 2026-06-26 — S4d-56/57/58 iOS PhotosPicker→render→export UI (accepted); S4d-58b docs closeout

- **S4d-56 (picker UI proof-or-block, accepted, `done/`):** with the iOS 27.0 runtime installed, probed whether the live `PhotosPicker → render → Share/Save` flow could be driven. **Block (tooling, not product):** no `idb`/`idb_companion`/`cliclick`/`fb-idb` installed (installing forbidden), `simctl` has **no** tap/touch/gesture/HID API (appearance-only `ui`), and the sim is booted **headless** (no Simulator.app window for `osascript`; `PHPickerViewController` is out-of-process). Non-invasively proven: `simctl addmedia`, app launch (no crash), and the picker entry UI + live shared witness rendered. Render engine stays proven by S4d-55.
- **S4d-57 (XCUITest driver, accepted as complete partial/blocked, `done/`):** added a minimal `iosAppUITests` XCUITest target (target + `PickerFlowUITests.swift` + scheme `<Testables>` + a `pickPhotoButton` accessibility id) — builds + runs via `xcodebuild test`. **New past S4d-56:** XCUITest **opens** the out-of-process PHPicker and renders its photo grid. **Stops at grid-cell selection:** on Xcode-27-beta/iOS-27 `collectionViews` is empty for the picker and `scrollViews.images` enumerates only the "Limited Access" banner icon (3 runs), so no photo is delivered and the app stays `.idle` (not `.failure`) — an accessibility-addressing limitation, not a render defect.
- **S4d-58 (UI-test fixture seam, accepted 2026-06-26):** added a `#if DEBUG`, `-uiTestFixtureImage`-gated seam in `ContentView` (compiled out of release; never fires on a normal launch) that generates a deterministic in-memory PNG and feeds it to the **unchanged** `WatermarkWorkflow.render` → `IosWatermarkRenderBridge` path (decode→render→encode — NOT a faked preview), bypassing **only** the blocked PHPicker selection step. XCUITest (`xcodebuild test`, iOS 27.0, **2 tests / 0 failures**) proved, with viewed screenshots: **watermarked preview** ("Watermarked 720×480, PNG 21,438 B", 315° Latin+CJK tiling) → **Save to Photos** ("Saved to Photos", add-only permission handled) → **Share** (system share sheet, watermarked PNG). **r1** hardened the asserts: Save now fails on "Save failed" and requires "Saved"; Share asserts the share sheet (`ActivityListView`/`Copy`) instead of discarding the wait. Edited only `iosApp/iosApp/ContentView.swift` + `iosApp/iosAppUITests/PickerFlowUITests.swift` (allowed scope); no shared/Android/dependency/golden change; no git mutation. **Real PHPicker grid-cell selection remains the one unproven step** (beta-toolchain/system-UI limit, bypassed by the seam, not claimed).
